67:             padding: 4px;
68:             flex-shrink: 0;
69:         }

73:             display: none;
74:         }

78:             scrollbar-width: none;
79:         }

83:             font-size: 0.75rem;
84:             text-transform: uppercase;
85:             letter-spacing: 0.05em;
86:             padding: 1rem;
87:         }
88:     </style>
89: </head>

636:                             </tr>
637:                         </tbody>
638:                     </table>
639:                 </div>
640:             </div>`;
641:         }
642: 
643:         function generateVectorComparison() {
644:             return `
645:             <div class="mt-8 glass rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-800">
646:                 <div class="p-4 border-b border-slate-800 bg-rose-500/5">

688:         // Render functions
689:         const navContainer = document.getElementById('sidebar-nav');
690:         const sectionsContainer = document.getElementById('stack-sections');
691: 
692:         techStack.forEach((section, index) => {
693:             // Sidebar Nav Item
694:             const navLink = document.createElement('a');
695:             navLink.href = `#${section.id}`;
696:             navLink.className = `sidebar-link group flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium rounded-lg transition-all ${index === 0 ? 'active' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'}`;
697:             navLink.innerHTML = `<i class="fas ${section.icon} w-5 text-center"></i> <span>${section.title}</span>`;
698:             navContainer.appendChild(navLink);
699: 
700:             // Section Block
701:             const sectionDiv = document.createElement('section');
702:             sectionDiv.id = section.id;
703:             sectionDiv.className = "scroll-mt-24";
704: 
705:             let itemsHtml = section.items.map(item => {
706:                 // Get actual URL from mapping, or generate fallback
707:                 const url = techUrls[item] || `https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(item)}`;
708:                 // Extract domain for favicon
709:                 let faviconDomain;
710:                 try {
711:                     faviconDomain = new URL(url).hostname;
712:                 } catch {
713:                     faviconDomain = item.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '') + ".com";
714:                 }
715:                 const logoUrl = `https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?sz=64&domain=${faviconDomain}`;
716: 
717:                 return `
718:                 <a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="tech-item p-3 flex items-center gap-4 rounded-xl border border-transparent hover:border-slate-700 cursor-pointer no-underline">
719:                     <div class="logo-box">
720:                         <img src="${logoUrl}" alt="${item}" class="max-w-full max-h-full" onerror="this.src='https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/2103/2103633.png'">
721:                     </div>
722:                     <span class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">${item}</span>
723:                     <i class="fas fa-external-link-alt external-icon text-slate-500 text-xs ml-auto"></i>
724:                 </a>`;
725:             }).join('');
726: 
727:             let extraGrid = "";
728:             if (section.id === "backend" || section.id === "languages") extraGrid = generateComparisonGrid();
729:             if (section.id === "vector") extraGrid = generateVectorComparison();
730: 
731:             sectionDiv.innerHTML = `
732:                 <div class="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
733:                     <div class="w-12 h-12 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center">
734:                         <i class="fas ${section.icon} text-sky-400 text-xl"></i>
735:                     </div>
736:                     <div>
737:                         <h3 class="text-2xl font-bold">${section.title}</h3>
738:                         <p class="text-slate-500 text-sm">Key infrastructure components for 2026 operations.</p>
739:                     </div>
740:                 </div>
741:                 <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
742:                     ${itemsHtml}
743:                 </div>
744:                 ${extraGrid}
745:             `;
746:             sectionsContainer.appendChild(sectionDiv);
747:         });
748: 
749:         // Intersection Observer for Sidebar Highlighting
750:         const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
751:             entries.forEach(entry => {
752:                 if (entry.isIntersecting) {
753:                     document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-link').forEach(link => {
754:                         link.classList.toggle('active', link.getAttribute('href') === `#${entry.target.id}`);
755:                     });
756:                 }
757:             });
758:         }, { threshold: 0.2 });
759: 
760:         document.querySelectorAll('section').forEach(section => observer.observe(section));
761: 
762:     </script>
763: </body>
764: 
765: </html>

(End of file - total 765 lines)
</content>

713: 00517|             <div class="highlight-summary">
714: 00518|                 Welcome to the VidiSmart Command Center: your direct gateway to the entire Gemini Ecosystem. We are
715: 00519|                 witnessing
716: 00520|                 a renaissance in AI, moving from simple chatbots to universal agents and world models. This dashboard
717: 00521|                 provides
718: 00522|                 instant access to Google's most advanced tools: from <strong>Project Astra</strong>, the future of
719: 00523|                 real-time
720: 00524|                 multimodal assistants, to <strong>Genie 3</strong>, which turns prompts into playable 3D worlds. Explore
721: 00525|                 scientific breakthroughs with <strong>AlphaFold 3</strong>, create cinema-quality video with
722: 00526|                 <strong>Veo</strong>,
723: 00527|                 and leverage the massive context window of <strong>Gemini 1.5 Pro</strong>. Everything you need to
724: 00528|                 build,
725: 00529|                 create, and discover is right here.

179:         .agent-icon {
180:             font-size: 2.5rem;
181:         }
182: 
183:         .tech-stack-grid {
184:             display: grid;
185:             grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
186:             gap: 1.5rem;
187:             margin: 2rem 0;
188:         }
189: 
190:         .tech-card {
191:             background: rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.8);
192:             border-radius: 16px;
193:             padding: 1.5rem;
194:             border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
195:         }
196: 
197:         .tech-card h4 {
198:             display: flex;
199:             align-items: center;
200:             gap: 0.5rem;
201:             margin-top: 0;
202:         }
203: 
204:         ul,
205:         ol {
206:             margin-left: 2rem;
207:             margin-bottom: 1rem;
208:         }
209: 
210:         li {
211:             margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
212:             color: #cbd5e1;
213:         }
214: 
215:         code {
216:             background: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
217:             padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
218:             border-radius: 6px;
219:             font-family: 'Fira Code', monospace;
220:             font-size: 0.9rem;
221:         }
222: 
223:         .highlight-box {
224:             background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1) 0%, rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.1) 100%);
225:             border-left: 4px solid var(--success);
226:             padding: 1.5rem;
227:             border-radius: 0 12px 12px 0;
228:             margin: 1.5rem 0;
229:         }
230: 
231:         .warning-box {
232:             background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1) 0%, rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1) 100%);
233:             border-left: 4px solid var(--warning);
234:             padding: 1.5rem;
235:             border-radius: 0 12px 12px 0;
236:             margin: 1.5rem 0;
237:         }
238: 
239:         .integration-flow {
240:             display: flex;
241:             flex-direction: column;
242:             gap: 1rem;
243:             margin: 2rem 0;
244:         }
245: 
246:         .flow-step {
247:             display: flex;
248:             align-items: center;
249:             gap: 1rem;
250:             padding: 1rem;
251:             background: rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.8);
252:             border-radius: 12px;
253:             border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
254:         }
255: 
256:         .flow-step-number {
257:             background: var(--primary);
258:             color: white;
259:             width: 32px;
260:             height: 32px;
261:             border-radius: 50%;
262:             display: flex;
263:             align-items: center;
264:             justify-content: center;
265:             font-weight: bold;
266:         }
267: 
268:         table {
269:             width: 100%;
270:             border-collapse: collapse;
271:             margin: 2rem 0;
272:             background: rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.5);
273:             border-radius: 12px;
274:             overflow: hidden;
275:         }
276: 
277:         th,
278:         td {
279:             padding: 1rem;
280:             text-align: left;
281:             border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
282:         }
283: 
284:         th {
285:             background: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.2);
286:             font-weight: 600;
287:             color: var(--primary);
288:         }
289: 
290:         tr:hover {
291:             background: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.05);
292:         }
293: 
294:         .page-break {
295:             page-break-after: always;
296:         }
297: 
298:         footer {
299:             text-align: center;
300:             padding: 3rem;
301:             background: rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.5);
302:             border-radius: 24px;
303:             margin-top: 4rem;
304:         }
305:

308:                 background: white;
309:                 color: black;
310:             }
311:

313:                 break-inside: avoid;
314:             }

316:     </style>
317: </head>
318: 
319: <body>
320:     <div class="container">
321:         <header>
322:             <h1>Smart Channel CX</h1>
323:             <p class="subtitle">AI-Powered Video Editing Platform Architecture Plan<br>
324:                 Multi-Agent System Design for Frame.io-Style Collaboration</p>
325:             <div class="badge-container">
326:                 <span class="badge">React/Next.js</span>
327:                 <span class="badge">Remotion</span>
328:                 <span class="badge">Cloudflare R2</span>
329:                 <span class="badge">ComfyUI</span>
330:                 <span class="badge">Gemini API</span>
331:                 <span class="badge">Rust/Astro</span>
332:                 <span class="badge">ONNX</span>
333:                 <span class="badge">Graph RAG</span>
334:             </div>
335:         </header>
336: 
337:         <nav>
338:             <ul>
339:                 <li><a href="#executive-summary">Executive Summary</a></li>
340:                 <li><a href="#system-overview">System Overview</a></li>
341:                 <li><a href="#multi-agent">Multi-Agent Architecture</a></li>
342:                 <li><a href="#tech-stack">Tech Stack</a></li>
343:                 <li><a href="#integrations">System Integrations</a></li>
344:                 <li><a href="#workflows">AI Workflows</a></li>
345:                 <li><a href="#roadmap">Implementation Roadmap</a></li>
346:             </ul>
347:         </nav>
348: 
349:         <!-- PAGE 1: Executive Summary -->
350:         <section id="executive-summary">
351:             <h2>01. Executive Summary</h2>
352: 
353:             <div class="highlight-box">
354:                 <strong>Mission:</strong> Build Smart Channel CX, a browser-based video editing platform with
355:                 Frame.io-style collaboration, powered by AI/ML pipelines and integrated with the existing Vidi ecosystem
356:                 (VidiFlow, VidiShop, VidiCity, Visual Vector Omni Search).
357:             </div>
358: 
359:             <h3>Project Vision</h3>
360:             <p>Smart Channel CX represents the convergence of professional video editing, AI-powered content generation,
361:                 and intelligent search capabilities. The platform enables users to edit MP4 files directly in the
362:                 browser while leveraging a powerful media pool stored in Cloudflare R2, with real-time collaboration
363:                 features inspired by Frame.io.</p>
364: 
365:             <h3>Key Capabilities</h3>
366:             <ul>
367:                 <li><strong>Browser-Based Video Editing:</strong> Full MP4 editing (trim, cut, split, transitions, text
368:                     overlays, audio mixing, filters) powered by Remotion</li>
369:                 <li><strong>AI-Powered Effects:</strong> Integration with ComfyUI and Stable Diffusion for generative
370:                     video effects</li>
371:                 <li><strong>Smart Graphics Generation:</strong> Gemini API integration for automated graphics and
372:                     thumbnail creation</li>
373:                 <li><strong>Visual Vector Omni Search:</strong> BM25 + Vector + Graph RAG + Geospatial search for
374:                     content discovery</li>
375:                 <li><strong>Frame.io-Style Collaboration:</strong> Time-stamped comments, version control, review
376:                     workflows</li>
377:                 <li><strong>Multi-Brand Support:</strong> VidiShop integration for personalized shopping experiences
378:                 </li>
379:                 <li><strong>Local News Portal:</strong> VidiCity geospatial video news platform integration</li>
380:                 <li><strong>Knowledge Base:</strong> AppFlowy (VidiFlow) integration for faceted content creation</li>
381:             </ul>
382: 
383:             <h3>Target Users</h3>
384:             <ul>
385:                 <li>Content creators and video editors</li>
386:                 <li>Marketing teams managing video campaigns</li>
387:                 <li>Local news organizations (VidiCity)</li>
388:                 <li>E-commerce businesses (VidiShop)</li>
389:                 <li>Agencies requiring client collaboration</li>
390:             </ul>
391:         </section>
392: 
393:         <!-- PAGE 2: System Overview -->
394:         <section id="system-overview">
395:             <h2>02. System Architecture Overview</h2>
396: 
397:             <h3>High-Level Architecture</h3>
398:             <div class="mermaid">
399:                 flowchart TB
400:                 subgraph "Client Layer"
401:                 WEB[Next.js 14 App Router]
402:                 EDITOR[Remotion Video Editor]
403:                 PLAYER[React Player Component]
404:                 end
405: 
406:                 subgraph "Edge Layer - Cloudflare"
407:                 CDN[Cloudflare CDN]
408:                 WORKERS[Cloudflare Workers]
409:                 R2[Cloudflare R2 Storage]
410:                 KV[Cloudflare KV]
411:                 D1[Cloudflare D1 Database]
412:                 end
413: 
414:                 subgraph "AI/ML Services"
415:                 COMFY[ComfyUI Server]
416:                 GEMINI[Google Gemini API]
417:                 ONNX[ONNX Runtime]
418:                 STABLE[Stable Diffusion]
419:                 end
420: 
421:                 subgraph "Search & Analytics"
422:                 VOS[Visual Vector Omni Search]
423:                 BM25[BM25 Keyword Engine]
424:                 VECTOR[Vector Database]
425:                 GRAPH[Graph RAG Neo4j]
426:                 GEO[Geospatial Search]
427:                 end
428: 
429:                 subgraph "Backend Services"
430:                 API[Rust/Astro API Gateway]
431:                 RENDER[Remotion Render Service]
432:                 COLLAB[Collaboration Service]
433:                 NOTIF[Notification Service]
434:                 end
435: 
436:                 subgraph "External Integrations"
437:                 APPFLOWY[AppFlowy/VidiFlow]
438:                 SHOPIFY[VidiShop/Shopify]
439:                 AUTH[Clerk Auth]
440:                 STRIPE[Stripe Payments]
441:                 end
442: 
443:                 WEB --> CDN
444:                 WEB --> WORKERS
445:                 EDITOR --> R2
446:                 PLAYER --> R2
447: 
448:                 WORKERS --> API
449:                 WORKERS --> VOS
450: 
451:                 API --> COMFY
452:                 API --> GEMINI
453:                 API --> ONNX
454:                 API --> RENDER
455:                 API --> COLLAB
456:                 API --> D1
457:                 API --> R2
458: 
459:                 VOS --> BM25
460:                 VOS --> VECTOR
461:                 VOS --> GRAPH
462:                 VOS --> GEO
463: 
464:                 COMFY --> STABLE
465:                 RENDER --> R2
466: 
467:                 API --> APPFLOWY
468:                 API --> SHOPIFY
469:                 API --> AUTH
470:                 API --> STRIPE
471:             </div>
472: 
473:             <h3>Data Flow Architecture</h3>
474:             <div class="mermaid">
475:                 sequenceDiagram
476:                 actor User
477:                 participant Browser as Next.js Editor
478:                 participant Edge as Cloudflare Edge
479:                 participant API as Rust API
480:                 participant AI as AI Services
481:                 participant R2 as Cloudflare R2
482:                 participant Search as Omni Search
483: 
484:                 User->>Browser: Upload MP4 to Media Pool
485:                 Browser->>Edge: POST /upload (presigned URL)
486:                 Edge->>R2: Direct Upload (multipart)
487:                 R2-->>Edge: Upload Complete
488:                 Edge->>API: Trigger Processing
489:                 API->>AI: Generate Thumbnails/Metadata
490:                 AI-->>API: AI Analysis Complete
491:                 API->>Search: Index Video Content
492:                 Search-->>API: Indexed
493:                 API-->>Browser: Video Ready
494: 
495:                 User->>Browser: Edit Video (trim/split)
496:                 Browser->>API: POST /edit/project
497:                 API->>R2: Read Source Segments
498:                 R2-->>API: Video Data
499:                 API->>API: Process Edit Operations
500:                 API->>R2: Store Edit Manifest
501:                 API-->>Browser: Preview URL
502:             </div>
503: 
504:             <h3>Key Architectural Decisions</h3>
505:             <table>
506:                 <tr>
507:                     <th>Decision</th>
508:                     <th>Choice</th>
509:                     <th>Rationale</th>
510:                 </tr>
511:                 <tr>
512:                     <td>Video Engine</td>
513:                     <td>Remotion + FFmpeg.wasm</td>
514:                     <td>React-based, programmable, browser-first</td>
515:                 </tr>
516:                 <tr>
517:                     <td>Storage</td>
518:                     <td>Cloudflare R2</td>
519:                     <td>S3-compatible, zero egress fees, edge-cached</td>
520:                 </tr>
521:                 <tr>
522:                     <td>Compute</td>
523:                     <td>Cloudflare Workers + Rust</td>
524:                     <td>Edge-deployed, low latency, WASM support</td>
525:                 </tr>
526:                 <tr>
527:                     <td>AI/ML</td>
528:                     <td>ComfyUI + ONNX</td>
529:                     <td>Modular pipelines, local/remote inference</td>
530:                 </tr>
531:                 <tr>
532:                     <td>Search</td>
533:                     <td>Hybrid Vector + BM25 + Graph</td>
534:                     <td>Best of all search paradigms</td>
535:                 </tr>
536:             </table>
537:         </section>
538: 
539:         <!-- PAGE 3: Multi-Agent Architecture -->
540:         <section id="multi-agent">
541:             <h2>03. Multi-Agent Architecture</h2>
542: 
543:             <p>The Smart Channel CX platform uses an agentic approach where specialized AI agents handle different
544:                 aspects of the video editing workflow. This enables parallel processing, intelligent automation, and
545:                 scalable collaboration.</p>
546: 
547:             <div class="mermaid">
548:                 flowchart LR
549:                 subgraph "Agent Orchestrator"
550:                 ORCH[Central Orchestrator<br />Rust-based Message Queue]
551:                 end
552: 
553:                 subgraph "Core Agents"
554:                 A1[Media Ingestion Agent]
555:                 A2[Edit Assistant Agent]
556:                 A3[Graphics Gen Agent]
557:                 A4[Collaboration Agent]
558:                 A5[Search Index Agent]
559:                 A6[Quality Control Agent]
560:                 A7[Export Render Agent]
561:                 end
562: 
563:                 subgraph "AI Tooling"
564:                 T1[ComfyUI]
565:                 T2[Gemini API]
566:                 T3[ONNX Models]
567:                 T4[Vector DB]
568:                 T5[Neo4j Graph]
569:                 end
570: 
571:                 ORCH --> A1
572:                 ORCH --> A2
573:                 ORCH --> A3
574:                 ORCH --> A4
575:                 ORCH --> A5
576:                 ORCH --> A6
577:                 ORCH --> A7
578: 
579:                 A1 --> T3
580:                 A2 --> T2
581:                 A3 --> T1
582:                 A3 --> T2
583:                 A4 --> T4
584:                 A5 --> T4
585:                 A5 --> T5
586:                 A6 --> T3
587:                 A7 --> T1
588:             </div>
589: 
590:             <h3>Agent Definitions</h3>
591: 
592:             <div class="agent-card">
593:                 <div class="agent-header">
594:                     <span class="agent-icon">📥</span>
595:                     <div>
596:                         <h4>Agent 01: Media Ingestion Agent</h4>
597:                         <code>media-ingestion-agent</code>
598:                     </div>
599:                 </div>
600:                 <p><strong>Responsibilities:</strong></p>
601:                 <ul>
602:                     <li>Handle MP4 uploads to Cloudflare R2 via presigned URLs</li>
603:                     <li>Extract metadata (duration, resolution, codec)</li>
604:                     <li>Generate multiple resolution variants (1080p, 4K)</li>
605:                     <li>Create thumbnail sprites for timeline preview</li>
606:                     <li>Transcode to browser-compatible formats</li>
607:                     <li>Trigger transcription for caption generation</li>
608:                 </ul>
609:                 <p><strong>Tools:</strong> FFmpeg.wasm, Whisper API, R2 SDK</p>
610:                 <p><strong>Triggers:</strong> Webhook on upload complete</p>
611:             </div>
612: 
613:             <div class="agent-card">
614:                 <div class="agent-header">
615:                     <span class="agent-icon">✂️</span>
616:                     <div>
617:                         <h4>Agent 02: Edit Assistant Agent</h4>
618:                         <code>edit-assistant-agent</code>
619:                     </div>
620:                 </div>
621:                 <p><strong>Responsibilities:</strong></p>
622:                 <ul>
623:                     <li>Process edit operations (trim, cut, split, merge)</li>
624:                     <li>Build edit decision lists (EDL) from user actions</li>
625:                     <li>Optimize render pipelines</li>
626:                     <li>Suggest edit points using scene detection</li>
627:                     <li>Apply smart transitions based on content analysis</li>
628:                 </ul>
629:                 <p><strong>Tools:</strong> Remotion Player, Scene Detection ONNX Model</p>
630:                 <p><strong>Triggers:</strong> User edit actions, auto-save intervals</p>
631:             </div>
632: 
633:             <div class="agent-card">
634:                 <div class="agent-header">
635:                     <span class="agent-icon">🎨</span>
636:                     <div>
637:                         <h4>Agent 03: Graphics Generation Agent</h4>
638:                         <code>graphics-gen-agent</code>
639:                     </div>
640:                 </div>
641:                 <p><strong>Responsibilities:</strong></p>
642:                 <ul>
643:                     <li>Generate AI-powered graphics from text prompts</li>
644:                     <li>Create animated lower thirds and titles</li>
645:                     <li>Generate thumbnail variations for A/B testing</li>
646:                     <li>Apply style transfer and visual effects via ComfyUI</li>
647:                     <li>Create branded templates for VidiShop products</li>
648:                 </ul>
649:                 <p><strong>Tools:</strong> Gemini API, ComfyUI API, Stable Diffusion</p>
650:                 <p><strong>Triggers:</strong> User prompt submission, template selection</p>
651:             </div>
652: 
653:             <div class="agent-card">
654:                 <div class="agent-header">
655:                     <span class="agent-icon">💬</span>
656:                     <div>
657:                         <h4>Agent 04: Collaboration Agent</h4>
658:                         <code>collaboration-agent</code>
659:                     </div>
660:                 </div>
661:                 <p><strong>Responsibilities:</strong></p>
662:                 <ul>
663:                     <li>Manage Frame.io-style comments with timecodes</li>
664:                     <li>Handle real-time collaboration sessions</li>
665:                     <li>Track version history and branching</li>
666:                     <li>Route notifications to team members</li>
667:                     <li>Generate share links with permissions</li>
668:                 </ul>
669:                 <p><strong>Tools:</strong> WebSockets, Cloudflare D1, Notification Service</p>
670:                 <p><strong>Triggers:</strong> Comment posts, version saves, share actions</p>
671:             </div>
672: 
673:             <div class="agent-card">
674:                 <div class="agent-header">
675:                     <span class="agent-icon">🔍</span>
676:                     <div>
677:                         <h4>Agent 05: Search Index Agent</h4>
678:                         <code>search-index-agent</code>
679:                     </div>
680:                 </div>
681:                 <p><strong>Responsibilities:</strong></p>
682:                 <ul>
683:                     <li>Extract visual embeddings from video frames</li>
684:                     <li>Index spoken content via transcription</li>
685:                     <li>Build knowledge graphs from content relationships</li>
686:                     <li>Enable geospatial tagging for VidiCity integration</li>
687:                     <li>Update vector indices in real-time</li>
688:                 </ul>
689:                 <p><strong>Tools:</strong> CLIP Model, Neo4j, Pinecone/Weaviate, PostGIS</p>
690:                 <p><strong>Triggers:</strong> Video processing complete, metadata updates</p>
691:             </div>
692: 
693:             <div class="agent-card">
694:                 <div class="agent-header">
695:                     <span class="agent-icon">✅</span>
696:                     <div>
697:                         <h4>Agent 06: Quality Control Agent</h4>
698:                         <code>quality-control-agent</code>
699:                     </div>
700:                 </div>
701:                 <p><strong>Responsibilities:</strong></p>
702:                 <ul>
703:                     <li>Validate export settings for target platforms</li>
704:                     <li>Check for audio/video sync issues</li>
705:                     <li>Verify color space and HDR compliance</li>
706:                     <li>Detect potential copyright issues in audio</li>
707:                     <li>Ensure accessibility standards (captions, descriptions)</li>
708:                 </ul>
709:                 <p><strong>Tools:</strong> FFmpeg validation, Audio fingerprinting ONNX models</p>
710:                 <p><strong>Triggers:</strong> Export request, pre-publish checks</p>
711:             </div>
712: 
713:             <div class="agent-card">
714:                 <div class="agent-header">
715:                     <span class="agent-icon">🎬</span>
716:                     <div>
717:                         <h4>Agent 07: Export Render Agent</h4>
718:                         <code>export-render-agent</code>
719:                     </div>
720:                 </div>
721:                 <p><strong>Responsibilities:</strong></p>
722:                 <ul>
723:                     <li>Queue and manage render jobs</li>
724:                     <li>Distribute rendering across worker nodes</li>
725:                     <li>Apply final compositing and effects</li>
726:                     <li>Generate multiple output formats simultaneously</li>
727:                     <li>Deliver to R2 and trigger distribution workflows</li>
728:                 </ul>
729:                 <p><strong>Tools:</strong> Remotion Lambda, FFmpeg, R2 SDK</p>
730:                 <p><strong>Triggers:</strong> Export button click, scheduled renders</p>
731:             </div>
732:         </section>
733: 
734:         <!-- PAGE 4: Tech Stack -->
735:         <section id="tech-stack">
736:             <h2>04. Technology Stack</h2>
737: 
738:             <h3>Frontend Layer</h3>
739:             <div class="tech-stack-grid">
740:                 <div class="tech-card">
741:                     <h4>⚛️ Next.js 14 (App Router)</h4>
742:                     <p>React framework with server components, streaming SSR, and edge runtime support. Handles the
743:                         video editor UI and real-time collaboration features.</p>
744:                 </div>
745:                 <div class="tech-card">
746:                     <h4>🎬 Remotion</h4>
747:                     <p>Programmable video editing in React. Core engine for timeline, preview, and rendering. Templates:
748:                         Recorder, Overlay, Next.js integration.</p>
749:                 </div>
750:                 <div class="tech-card">
751:                     <h4>🎨 TailwindCSS + Framer Motion</h4>
752:                     <p>Utility-first styling and animations for the editor interface. Frame.io-inspired dark theme with
753:                         smooth interactions.</p>
754:                 </div>
755:                 <div class="tech-card">
756:                     <h4>📹 FFmpeg.wasm</h4>
757:                     <p>Browser-based video processing for client-side operations, thumbnail generation, and format
758:                         validation.</p>
759:                 </div>
760:             </div>
761: 
762:             <h3>Edge & Serverless Layer</h3>
763:             <div class="tech-stack-grid">
764:                 <div class="tech-card">
765:                     <h4>☁️ Cloudflare Workers</h4>
766:                     <p>Edge compute for API routing, authentication, and real-time WebSocket handling. Deployed globally
767:                         for minimal latency.</p>
768:                 </div>
769:                 <div class="tech-card">
770:                     <h4>🦀 Rust + Astro</h4>
771:                     <p>High-performance API gateway and compute services. WASM-compatible for running ONNX models at the
772:                         edge.</p>
773:                 </div>
774:                 <div class="tech-card">
775:                     <h4>🗄️ Cloudflare D1</h4>
776:                     <p>SQLite-based edge database for projects, user data, and collaboration state. Replicated globally.
777:                     </p>
778:                 </div>
779:                 <div class="tech-card">
780:                     <h4>⚡ Cloudflare KV</h4>
781:                     <p>Key-value store for session data, cache, and real-time collaboration state.</p>
782:                 </div>
783:             </div>
784: 
785:             <h3>Storage & CDN</h3>
786:             <div class="tech-stack-grid">
787:                 <div class="tech-card">
788:                     <h4>📦 Cloudflare R2</h4>
789:                     <p>S3-compatible object storage for video files, assets, and renders. Zero egress fees, ideal for
790:                         media delivery.</p>
791:                 </div>
792:                 <div class="tech-card">
793:                     <h4>🌐 Cloudflare CDN</h4>
794:                     <p>Global content delivery for video segments, thumbnails, and static assets. Smart caching
795:                         strategies.</p>
796:                 </div>
797:                 <div class="tech-card">
798:                     <h4>🔄 TUS Protocol</h4>
799:                     <p>Resumable upload protocol for large video files. Enables pause/resume and parallel chunk uploads.
800:                     </p>
801:                 </div>
802:             </div>
803: 
804:             <h3>AI/ML Services</h3>
805:             <div class="tech-stack-grid">
806:                 <div class="tech-card">
807:                     <h4>🧠 ComfyUI</h4>
808:                     <p>Node-based Stable Diffusion interface for AI video effects, style transfer, and generative
